Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the data_structures structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 456. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 259 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the data_structures decomposition. By minimality, the element a^456 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 259-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
